# Places (user decision 2026-11-10: pixel art): 256×96 pictures for story pages, events and the battle backdrop, the horizon on
# row 64 and plain ground below it for the party to walk on; 'title' is the title screen (256×144). '!name' starts a picture;
# letters are palette.csv letters. The game crops the sides or the top to fit, and draws snow, ash, firelight and the party on top.
